Callers should treat timeout and error as non-fatal — log\n53\t    /// and continue rather than blocking the worker in HANDLING state.\n54\t    async fn timeout_op<T, F, Fut>(\n55\t        &self,\n56\t        op: F,\n57\t        operation_name: &str,\n58\t    ) -> Result<Option<T>, anyhow::Error>\n59\t    where\n60\t        F: FnOnce() -> Fut,\n61\t        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = Result<T, anyhow::Error>>,\n62\t    {\n63\t        match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), op()).await {\n64\t            Ok(Ok(result)) => Ok(Some(result)),\n65\t            Ok(Err(e)) => Err(e),\n66\t            Err(_) => {\n67\t                tracing::error!(\n68\t                    operation = operation_name,\n69\t                    \"bead store operation timed out after 30s\"\n70\t                );\n71\t                Ok(None)\n72\t            }\n73\t        }\n74\t    }\n75\t\n76\t    /// Release a bead with sync conflict recovery and graceful failure handling.\n77\t    ///\n78\t    /// Flow:\n79\t    /// 1. If SYNC_CONFLICT recovery fails, emit `BeadReleaseFailed` and return Ok.\n82\t    ///\n83\t    /// This method never returns an error — release failures are non-fatal.\n84\t    /// The worker can         });\n106\t            }\n107\t            Ok(None) => {\n108\t                tracing::warn!(\n109\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n110\t                    \"flush timed out before });\n118\t            }\n119\t            Err(e) => {\n120\t                tracing::warn!(\n121\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n122\t                    error = %e,\n123\t       });\n131\t            }\n132\t        }\n133\t\n134\t        // Step 2: Attempt release with timeout protection.\n135\t        // Emit heartbeat before br release call to track where hang occurs.\n136\t        let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted {\n137\t            bead_id: Some(bead.id.clone()),\n138\t            state:           });\n155\t                events.push(EventKind::BeadReleased {\n156\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n157\t                    reason: \"release_success\".to_string(),\n158\t        Err(_) => {\n183\t                // Timeout after 30s.\n184\t                tracing::error!(\n185\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n186\t                    \"br release timed out after 30s — worker will continue (bead may remain assigned)\"\n187\t                );\n188\t                events.push(EventKind::BeadReleaseFailed {\n189\t                    bead_id: Every `Outcome` variant has an explicit arm —\n202\t    /// no wildcards. Returns a `HandlerResult` describing what happened.\n203\t    #[tracing::instrument(\n204\t        name = \"bead.outcome\",\n205\t        skip(self, store, bead, output),\n206\t        fields(\n207\t pub async fn handle(\n213\t        &self,\n214\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n215\t        bead: &Bead,\n216\t        output: &AgentOutcome,\n217\t        was_interrupted: bool,\n218\t    ) -> tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.outcome\", outcome.as_str());\n223\t\n224\t        tracing::info!(\n225\t            bead_id = %bead.id,\n226\t            exit_code = output.exit_code,\n227\t // This prevents worker hang in HANDLING state when telemetry is wedged.\n233\t        let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::OutcomeClassified {\n234\t            bead_id: Outcome::Timeout => self.handle_timeout(store, bead).await?,\n243\t            Outcome::AgentNotFound => self.handle_agent_not_found(store, bead).await?,\n244\t            Outcome::Interrupted => self.handle_interrupted(store, bead).await?,\n245\t            Outcome::Crash(code) => self.handle_crash(store, bead, code).await?,\n246\t        };\n247\t\n248\t        // Emit sub-handler events (e.g. BeadCompleted, BeadOrphaned) to the\n249\t        // telemetry sink so they appear in the JSONL log.\n250\t        // Use emit_try_lock() to avoid blocking if telemetry writer is stuck.\n251\t        for event in &telemetry_events {\n252\t            let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(event.clone());\n253\t        }\n254\t\n255\t        // Use emit_try_lock() to avoid blocking if telemetry writer is stuck.\n256\t        let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::OutcomeHandled {\n257\t            bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n258\t            outcome: outcome.as_str().to_string(),\n259\t            action: bead_action.to_string(),\n260\t        });\n261\t\n262\t        // Set action as span attribute\n263\t        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.outcome.action\", bead_action.to_string());\n264\t\n265\t        // Set span status: Ok for success, Error otherwise\n266\t        if !matches!(outcome, Outcome::Success) {\n267\t            tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_code\", 2u64);\n268\t            tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_description\", outcome.as_str());\n269\t        }\n270\t\n271\t        Ok(HandlerResult {\n272\t            outcome,\n273\t            bead_action,\n274\t            telemetry_events,\n275\t        })\n276\t    }\n277\t\n278\t    /// Handle a process output with cancellation support.\n279\t    ///\n280\t    /// Checks the cancellation flag before starting the handler and returns\n281\t    /// early if the handler has been cancelled (e.g., due to a timeout in\n282\t    /// the worker). This prevents the handler from making further br calls\n283\t    /// after a timeout has occurred.\n284\t    pub async fn handle_with_cancellation(\n285\t        &self,\n286\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n287\t        bead: &Bead,\n288\t        output: &AgentOutcome,\n289\t        was_interrupted: bool,\n290\t        cancelled: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n291\t    ) -> Result<HandlerResult> {\n292\t        // Check if we've been cancelled before starting.\n293\t        if cancelled.load(Ordering::Acquire) {\n294\t            tracing::warn!(\n295\t                bead_id = %bead.id,\n296\t                \"outcome handler cancelled before starting, returning early\"\n297\t            );\n298\t            // Return a default HandlerResult to allow the worker to recover.\n299\t            return Ok(HandlerResult {\n300\t                outcome: classify(output.exit_code, was_interrupted),\n301\t                bead_action: BeadAction::None,\n302\t                telemetry_events: vec![],\n303\t            });\n304\t        }\n305\t\n306\t        // Wrap the handler in a timeout to prevent indefinite hangs.\n307\t        // This is a safety net in case the internal br call timeouts don't work.\n308\t        let bead_id = bead.id.clone();\n309\t        let telemetry = self.telemetry.clone();\n310\t\n311\t        match tokio::time::timeout(\n312\t            std::time::Duration::from_secs(50),\n313\t            self.handle(store, bead, output, was_interrupted),\n314\t        )\n315\t        .await\n316\t        {\n317\t            Ok(Ok(result)) => Ok(result),\n318\t            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n319\t                // Handler returned an error.\n320\t                tracing::error!(\n321\t                    bead_id = %bead_id,\n322\t                    error = %e,\n323\t                    \"outcome handler returned error\"\n324\t                );\n325\t                Err(e)\n326\t            }\n327\t            Err(_) => {\n328\t                // Timeout after 50 seconds.\n329\t                tracing::error!(\n330\t                    bead_id = %bead_id,\n331\t                    \"outcome handler timed out after 50s, returning early to allow worker recovery\"\n332\t                );\n333\t                // Emit a timeout event for observability.\n334\t                let _ = telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n335\t                    bead_id: bead_id.clone(),\n336\t                    outcome: classify(output.exit_code, was_interrupted)\n337\t                        .as_str()\n338\t                        .to_string(),\n339\t                    operation: \"handle\".to_string(),\n340\t                    error: \"timeout after 50s\".to_string(),\n341\t                });\n342\t                // Return a default HandlerResult to allow the worker to recover.\n343\t                // The worker's 60-second timeout wrapper will handle best-effort release.\n344\t                Ok(HandlerResult {\n345\t                    outcome: classify(output.exit_code, was_interrupted),\n346\t                    bead_action: BeadAction::Released,\n347\t                    telemetry_events: vec![EventKind::BeadReleased {\n348\t                        bead_id,\n349\t                        reason: \"handler_timeout\".to_string(),\n350\t                    }],\n351\t                })\n352\t            }\n353\t        }\n354\t    }\n355\t\n356\t    /// Success: run validation gates (if configured), then verify bead closure.\n357\t    ///\n358\t    /// Flow:\n359\t    /// 1. If validation gates are configured (new or legacy format), run them.\n360\t    /// 2. If any gate fails: reopen the bead (if agent closed it) and release it.\n361\t    /// 3. If all gates pass (or none configured): check if agent closed the bead.\n362\t    ///    - Closed → emit BeadCompleted.\n363\t    ///    - Still open → emit BeadOrphaned warning.\n364\t    ///\n365\t    /// NEEDLE does NOT auto-close — the agent owns closure via `br close`.\n366\t    async fn handle_success(\n367\t        &self,\n368\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n369\t        bead: &Bead,\n370\t    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n371\t        tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent completed successfully\");\n372\t\n373\t        // Try pluggable gates first, fall back to legacy verification commands.\n374\t        let gate = if !self.config.gates.is_empty() {\n375\t            // New pluggable gate system.\n376\t            let gate_configs: Vec<(String, GateConfig)> = self\n377\t                .config\n378\t                .gates\n379\t                .iter()\n380\t                .enumerate()\n381\t                .map(|(i, config)| (format!(\"gate_{}\", i), config.clone()))\n382\t                .collect();\n383\t            ValidationGate::new(gate_configs, bead.workspace.clone())\n384\t        } else if !self.config.verification.is_empty() {\n385\t            // Legacy verification command format.\n386\t            ValidationGate::from_commands(self.config.verification.clone(), bead.workspace.clone())\n387\t        } else {\n388\t            None\n389\t        };\n390\t\n391\t        if let Some(gate) = gate {\n392\t            let report = gate.run(bead).await?;\n393\t\n394\t            if !report.all_passed {\n395\t                return self.handle_gate_failure(store, bead, &report).await;\n396\t            }\n397\t\n398\t            // All gates passed — emit telemetry.\n399\t            let gates_run = report.results.len() as u32;\n400\t            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::VerificationPassed {\n401\t                bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n402\t                gates_run,\n403\t            })?;\n404\t            tracing::info!(\n405\t                bead_id = %bead.id,\n406\t                gates_run,\n407\t                \"all validation gates passed\"\n408\t            );\n409\t        }\n410\t\n411\t        // Reset failure count on successful gate completion.\n412\t        // This ensures the bead starts fresh on the next cycle if re-claimed.\n413\t        let _ = self.reset_failure_count(store, bead).await;\n414\t\n415\t        // Normal success flow: check if agent closed the bead.\n416\t        let mut events = Vec::new();\n417\t\n418\t        // Use timeout for show() to prevent indefinite hang in HANDLING state.\n419\t        match self.timeout_op(|| store.show(&bead.id), \"show\").await {\n420\t            Ok(Some(current)) if current.status.is_done() => {\n421\t                tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"bead confirmed closed by agent\");\n422\t                events.push(EventKind::BeadCompleted {\n423\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n424\t                    duration_ms: 0,\n425\t                });\n426\t                // Increment success_count for any skills that matched this bead.\n427\t                if !bead.workspace.as_os_str().is_empty() {\n428\t                    if let Ok(lib) = crate::skill::SkillLibrary::load(&bead.workspace) {\n429\t                        if let Err(e) = lib.increment_success_for_bead(&bead.labels, &bead.title) {\n430\t                            tracing::warn!(\n431\t                                bead_id = %bead.id,\n432\t                                error = %e,\n433\t                                \"failed to increment skill success counts\"\n434\t                            );\n435\t                        }\n436\t                    }\n437\t                }\n438\t            }\n439\t            Ok(Some(current)) => {\n440\t                tracing::warn!(\n441\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n442\t                    status = %current.status,\n443\t                    \"agent exited successfully but bead is still open (orphaned)\"\n444\t                );\n445\t                events.push(EventKind::BeadOrphaned {\n446\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n447\t                });\n448\t            }\n449\t            Ok(None) => {\n450\t                // Timeout - emit telemetry and continue.\n451\t                tracing::warn!(\n452\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n453\t                    \"show() timed out, could not verify bead closure status\"\n454\t                );\n455\t                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n456\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n457\t                    outcome: \"success\".to_string(),\n458\t                    operation: \"show\".to_string(),\n459\t                    error: \"timeout after 30s\".to_string(),\n460\t                });\n461\t            }\n462\t            Err(e) => {\n463\t                tracing::warn!(\n464\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n465\t                    error = %e,\n466\t                    \"could not verify bead closure status\"\n467\t                );\n468\t                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n469\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n470\t                    outcome: \"success\".to_string(),\n471\t                    operation: \"show\".to_string(),\n472\t                    error: e.to_string(),\n473\t                });\n474\t            }\n475\t        }\n476\t\n477\t        // Flush SQLite state to JSONL so the closed/orphaned bead is captured in\n478\t        // the git-committed backup. Non-fatal: a flush failure is logged but does\n479\t        // not block the worker from picking up the next bead.\n480\t        match self.timeout_op(|| store.flush(), \"flush\").await {\n481\t            Ok(Some(())) => {\n482\t                tracing::debug!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"flushed bead state to JSONL after success\");\n483\t            }\n484\t            Ok(None) => {\n485\t                tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"flush timed out after success — JSONL may lag SQLite\");\n486\t            }\n487\t            Err(e) => {\n488\t                tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, error = %e, \"flush failed after success — JSONL may lag SQLite\");\n489\t            }\n490\t        }\n491\t\n492\t        Ok((BeadAction::None, events))\n493\t    }\n494\t\n495\t    /// Handle gate failure: reopen the bead if it was closed, then release it.\n496\t    async fn handle_gate_failure(\n497\t        &self,\n498\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n499\t        bead: &Bead,\n500\t        report: &crate::validation::GateReport,\n501\t    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n502\t        // Find the first failing gate for telemetry.\n503\t        let (failed_gate, reason) = report\n504\t            .results\n505\t            .iter()\n506\t            .find(|(_, r)| !r.passed())\n507\t            .map(|(name, r)| (name.clone(), bead_id = %bead.id,\n512\t            gate = %failed_gate,\n513\t            reason = %reason,\n514\t            \"validation gate failed — releasing bead\"\n515\t        );\n516\t\n517\t        // already closed the bead, reopen it before releasing.\n528\t        // Use timeout to prevent indefinite hang in HANDLING state.\n529\t        match self.timeout_op(|| store.show(&bead.id), \"show\").await {\n530\t            Ok(Some(current)) if current.status.is_done() => {\n531\t                tracing::info!(\n532\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n533\t Ok(_) => {}\n537\t                    Err(e) => {\n538\t                        tracing::warn!(\n539\t                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n540\t                            error = %e,\n541\t                            \"failed to reopen bead — attempting release anyway\"\n542\t                        );\n543\t                        events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n544\t                            bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n545\t                            outcome: \"gate_failure\".to_string(),\n546\t                            operation: \"reopen\".to_string(),\n547\t                            error: e.to_string(),\n548\t                        });\n549\t                    }\n550\t                }\n551\t            }\n552\t            Ok(None) => {\n553\t                tracing::warn!(\n554\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n555\t                    \"show() timed out during gate failure handling, skipping reopen\"\n556\t                );\n557\t                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n558\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n559\t                    outcome: \"gate_failure\".to_string(),\n560\t                    operation: \"show\".to_string(),\n561\t                    error: \"timeout after 30s\".to_string(),\n562\t                });\n563\t            }\n564\t            Err(e) => {\n565\t                tracing::warn!(\n566\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n567\t                    error = %e,\n568\t                    \"show() failed during gate failure handling\"\n569\t                );\n570\t            }\n571\t            _ => {}\n572\t        }\n573\t\n574\t        // Release the bead back to open with flush-before-release and sync recovery.\n575\t        let mut release_events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n576\t        events.append(&mut release_events);\n577\t\n578\t        // If release succeeded, increment failure count.\n579\t        let release_succeeded = events\n580\t            .iter()\n581\t            .any(|e| matches!(e, EventKind::BeadReleased { .. }));\n582\t        if release_succeeded {\n583\t            if let Err(e) = self.increment_failure_count(store, bead).await {\n584\t                tracing::warn!(\n585\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n586\t                    error = %e,\n587\t                    \"failed to increment failure count after gate failure\"\n588\t                );\n589\t            }\n590\t        }\n591\t\n592\t        // Add a label indicating verification failure.\n593\t        if let Err(e) = tokio::time::timeout(\n594\t            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n595\t            store.add_label(&bead.id, \"verification-failed\"),\n596\t        )\n597\t        .await\n598\t        {\n599\t            tracing::warn!(\n600\t                bead_id = %bead.id,\n601\t                error = %e,\n602\t                \"failed to add verification-failed label\"\n603\t            );\n604\t        }\n605\t\n606\t        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n607\t    }\n608\t\n609\t    /// Failure: release bead and increment failure count.\n610\t    ///\n611\t    /// Mitosis evaluation (for multi-task splitting) is handled externally by\n612\t    /// the worker after outcome handling — see `MitosisEvaluator`.\n613\t    ///\n614\t    /// If br calls timeout or fail, logs the error and continues — does not\n615\t    /// block the worker in HANDLING state indefinitely.\n616\t    async fn handle_failure(\n617\t        &self,\n618\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n619\t        bead: &Bead,\n620\t    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n621\t        tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent failure — releasing bead\");\n622\t\n623\t        let mut events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n624\t\n625\t        // If release succeeded, increment failure count.\n626\t        // Check if the last event is BeadReleased (success) or BeadReleaseFailed (failure).\n627\t        let release_succeeded = events\n628\t            .iter()\n629\t            .any(|e| matches!(e, EventKind::BeadReleased { .. }));\n630\t        if release_succeeded {\n631\t            if let Err(e) = self.increment_failure_count(store, bead).await {\n632\t                tracing::warn!(\n633\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n634\t                    error = %e,\n635\t                    \"failed to increment failure count after release\"\n636\t                );\n637\t            }\n638\t        }\n639\t\n640\t        // Ensure we emit a reason event for telemetry.\n641\t        if !events.iter().any(|e| {\n642\t            matches!(\n643\t           bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n649\t                reason: \"failure\".to_string(),\n650\t            });\n651\t        }\n652\t\n653\t        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n654\t    }\n655\t\n656\t    /// Timeout: release bead and add `deferred` label.\n657\t    ///\n658\t    /// If br calls timeout or fail, logs the error and continues — does not\n659\t    /// block the worker in HANDLING state indefinitely.\n660\t    async fn handle_timeout(\n661\t        &self,\n662\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n663\t        bead: &Bead,\n664\t    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n665\t        tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent timed out — releasing bead as deferred\");\n666\t\n667\t        let mut events = self.release_bead(store, .any(|e| matches!(e, EventKind::BeadReleased { .. }));\n673\t        if release_succeeded {\n674\t            // Increment failure count for auto-split tracking.\n675\t            if let Err(e) = self.increment_failure_count(store, bead).await {\n676\t                tracing::warn!(\n677\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n678\t                    error = %e,\n679\t             );\n694\t                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n695\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n696\t                    outcome: }\n702\t\n703\t        Ok((BeadAction::Deferred, events))\n704\t    }\n705\t\n706\t    /// Crash: release bead and create alert bead with diagnostic info.\n707\t    async fn handle_crash(\n708\t        &self,\n709\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n710\t        bead: &Bead,\n711\t        signal_code: i32,\n712\t    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n713\t        tracing::error!(\n714\t            bead_id = %bead.id,\n715\t            signal_code,\n716\t            agent = %self.config.agent.default,\n717\t            \"agent crashed — releasing bead and creating alert\"\n718\t        );\n719\t\n720\t        let events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n721\t\n722\t        // Create alert bead with diagnostic info (best-effort).\n723\t        let signal_num = if signal_code > 128 {\n724\t            signal_code - 128\n725\t        } else {\n726\t            signal_code\n727\t        };\n728\t        let timestamp = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();\n729\t        let alert_title = format!(\"ALERT: Agent crash on bead {}\", bead.id);\n730\t        let alert_body = format!(\n731\t            \"## Agent Crash Report\\n\\\n732\t             \\n\\\n733\t             - **Bead ID**: {}\\n\\\n734\t             - **Agent**: {}\\n\\\n735\t             - **Exit code**: {} (signal {})\\n\\\n736\t             - **Workspace**: {}\\n\\\n737\t             - **Timestamp**: {}\\n\\\n738\t             \\n\\\n739\t             The agent process was killed. This bead has been released for retry.\",\n740\t            bead.id,\n741\t            self.config.agent.default,\n742\t            signal_code,\n743\t            signal_num,\n744\t            bead.workspace.display(),\n745\t            timestamp,\n746\t        );\n747\t\n748\t        // Hook 4: propagate stitch labels from the crashed bead to the alert.\n749\t        let signal_label = format!(\"signal-{}\", signal_num);\n750\t        let mut alert_labels: Vec<String> = vec![\"alert\".into(), \"crash\".into(), signal_label];\n751\t        alert_labels.extend(crate::types::extract_stitch_labels(&bead.labels));\n752\t        let alert_label_refs: Vec<&str> = alert_labels.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();\n753\t        match tokio::time::timeout(\n754\t            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n755\t            store.create_bead(&alert_title, &alert_body, &alert_label_refs),\n756\t        )\n757\t        .await\n758\t        {\n759\t            Ok(Ok(alert_id)) => {\n760\t                tracing::info!(\n761\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n762\t                    %alert_id,\n763\t                    \"crash alert bead created\"\n764\t                );\n765\t            }\n766\t            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n767\t                tracing::warn!(\n768\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n769\t                    error = %e,\n770\t                    \"failed to create crash alert bead\"\n771\t                );\n772\t            }\n773\t            Err(_) => {\n774\t                tracing::warn!(\n775\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n776\t                    \"create_bead timed out after 30s during crash handling\"\n777\t                );\n778\t            }\n779\t        }\n780\t\n781\t        Ok((BeadAction::Alerted, events))\n782\t    }\n783\t\n784\t    /// AgentNotFound: release bead, emit error. No retry — this is a config issue.\n785\t    async fn handle_agent_not_found(\n786\t        &self,\n787\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n788\t        bead: &Bead,\n789\t    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n790\t        tracing::error!(\n791\t            bead_id = %bead.id,\n792\t            agent = %self.config.agent.default,\n793\t            \"agent binary not found — releasing bead (config issue, no retry)\"\n794\t        );\n795\t\n796\t        let events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n797\t        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n798\t    }\n799\t\n800\t    /// Interrupted: release bead for graceful shutdown.\n801\t    async fn handle_interrupted(\n802\t        &self,\n803\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n804\t        bead: &Bead,\n805\t    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n806\t        tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent interrupted — releasing bead for clean shutdown\");\n807\t\n808\t        let events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n809\t        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n810\t    }\n811\t\n812\t    /// Increment the failure count label on a bead.\n813\t    ///\n814\t    /// Labels follow the pattern `failure-count:N`. If `failure-count:2` exists,\n815\t    /// the old label is removed and `failure-count:3` is added.\n816\t    ///\n817\t    /// All `br` calls are wrapped in timeouts to prevent indefinite hang in\n818\t    /// HANDLING state. Failures are non-fatal — we log and continue.\n819\t    async fn increment_failure_count(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore, bead: &Bead) -> Result<()> {\n820\t        // Read labels with timeout.\n821\t        let labels =\n822\t            match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), store.labels(&bead.id))\n823\t                .await\n824\t            {\n825\t                Ok(Ok(l)) => l,\n826\t                Ok(Err(e)) => {\n827\t                    tracing::warn!(\n828\t                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n829\t                        error = %e,\n830\t                        \"could not read labels to increment failure count\"\n831\t                    );\n832\t                    return Ok(());\n833\t                }\n834\t                Err(_) => {\n835\t                    tracing::warn!(\n836\t                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n837\t                        \"labels() timed out after 30s, skipping failure count increment\"\n838\t                    );\n839\t                    return Ok(());\n840\t                }\n841\t            };\n842\t\n843\t        let current_count = labels\n844\t            .iter()\n845\t            .filter_map(|l| l.strip_prefix(\"failure-count:\"))\n846\t            .filter_map(|n| n.parse::<u32>().ok())\n847\t            .max()\n848\t            .unwrap_or(0);\n849\t\n850\t        let new_count = current_count + 1;\n851\t        let new_label = format!(\"failure-count:{}\", new_count);\n852\t\n853\t        // Remove old failure-count labels before adding the new one.\n854\t        // Each remove_label call is wrapped in a timeout.\n855\t        for label in &labels {\n856\t            if label.starts_with(\"failure-count:\") {\n857\t                match tokio::time::timeout(\n858\t                    std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n859\t                    store.remove_label(&bead.id, label),\n860\t                )\n861\t                .await\n862\t                {\n863\t                    Ok(Ok(())) => {}\n864\t                    Ok(Err(e)) => {\n865\t                        tracing::warn!(\n866\t                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n867\t                            label,\n868\t                            error = %e,\n869\t                            \"failed to remove old failure-count label\"\n870\t                        );\n871\t                    }\n872\t                    Err(_) => {\n873\t                        tracing::warn!(\n874\t                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n875\t                            label,\n876\t                            \"remove_label timed out after 30s\"\n877\t                        );\n878\t                    }\n879\t                }\n880\t            }\n881\t        }\n882\t\n883\t        // Add the new label with timeout.\n884\t        match tokio::time::timeout(\n885\t            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n886\t            store.add_label(&bead.id, &new_label),\n887\t        )\n888\t        .await\n889\t        {\n890\t            Ok(Ok(())) => {\n891\t                tracing::debug!(\n892\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n893\t                    count = new_count,\n894\t                    \"failure count incremented\"\n895\t                );\n896\t            }\n897\t            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n898\t                tracing::warn!(\n899\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n900\t                    error = %e,\n901\t                    \"failed to add failure-count label\"\n902\t                );\n903\t            }\n904\t            Err(_) => {\n905\t                tracing::warn!(\n906\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n907\t                    \"add_label timed out after 30s\"\n908\t                );\n909\t            }\n910\t        }\n911\t\n912\t        Ok(())\n913\t    }\n914\t\n915\t    /// Reset the failure count label on a bead by removing all `failure-count:N` labels.\n916\t    ///\n917\t    /// Called on success to clear the failure counter so the bead starts fresh\n918\t    /// on the next cycle.\n919\t    ///\n920\t    /// All `br` calls are wrapped in timeouts to prevent indefinite hang in\n921\t    /// HANDLING state. Failures are non-fatal — we log and continue.\n922\t    async fn reset_failure_count(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore, bead: &Bead) -> Result<()> {\n923\t        // Read labels with timeout.\n924\t        let labels =\n925\t            match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), store.labels(&bead.id))\n926\t                .await\n927\t            {\n928\t                Ok(Ok(l)) => l,\n929\t                Ok(Err(e)) => {\n930\t                    tracing::warn!(\n931\t                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n932\t                        error = %e,\n933\t                        \"could not read labels to reset failure count\"\n934\t                    );\n935\t                    return Ok(());\n936\t                }\n937\t                Err(_) => {\n938\t                    tracing::warn!(\n939\t                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n940\t                        \"labels() timed out after 30s, skipping failure count reset\"\n941\t                    );\n942\t                    return Ok(());\n943\t                }\n944\t            };\n945\t\n946\t        // Remove all failure-count labels.\n947\t        let mut removed_count = 0;\n948\t        for label in &labels {\n949\t            if label.starts_with(\"failure-count:\") {\n950\t                match tokio::time::timeout(\n951\t                    std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n952\t                    store.remove_label(&bead.id, label),\n953\t                )\n954\t                .await\n955\t                {\n956\t                    Ok(Ok(())) => {\n957\t                        removed_count += 1;\n958\t                    }\n959\t                    Ok(Err(e)) => {\n960\t                        tracing::warn!(\n961\t                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n962\t                            label,\n963\t                            error = %e,\n964\t                            \"failed to remove failure-count label\"\n965\t                        );\n966\t                    }\n967\t                    Err(_) => {\n968\t                        tracing::warn!(\n969\t                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n970\t                            label,\n971\t                            \"remove_label timed out after 30s\"\n972\t                        );\n973\t                    }\n974\t                }\n975\t            }\n976\t        }\n977\t\n978\t        if removed_count > 0 {\n979\t            tracing::debug!(\n980\t                bead_id = %bead.id,\n981\t                removed_count,\n982\t                \"failure count reset\"\n983\t            );\n984\t        }\n985\t\n986\t        Ok(())\n987\t    }\n988\t}\n989\t\n990\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n991\t// Outcome Display\n992\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n993\t\n994\timpl fmt::Display for Outcome {\n995\t    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n996\t        match self {\n997\t            Outcome::Success => write!(f, \"Success\"),\n998\t            Outcome::Failure => write!(f, \"Failure\"),\n999\t            Outcome::Timeout => write!(f, \"Timeout\"),\n1000\t            Outcome::AgentNotFound => write!(f, \"AgentNotFound\"),\n1001\t            Outcome::Interrupted => write!(f, \"Interrupted\"),\n1002\t            Outcome::Crash(code) => write!(f, \"Crash({})\", code),\n1003\t        }\n1004\t    }\n1005\t}\n1006\t\n1007\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n1008\t// Tests\n1009\t// Outcome routing: map agent exit codes to explicit handlers.\n//!\n//! Every possible exit code has a named handler. The type system enforces\n//! exhaustiveness — if an outcome can happen, it must have a handler.\n//!\n//! Depends on: `types`, `config`, `bead_store`, `telemetry`, `validation`.\n\nuse std::fmt;\nuse std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};\nuse std::sync::Arc;\n\nuse anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::Utc;\n\nuse crate::bead_store::{BeadStore, SyncRecoveryError};\nuse crate::config::Config;\nuse crate::telemetry::{EventKind, Telemetry};\n#[cfg(test)]\nuse crate::types::BeadStatus;\nuse crate::types::{AgentOutcome, Bead, BeadAction, HandlerResult, Outcome};\nuse crate::validation::{GateConfig, ValidationGate};\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// classify (convenience re-export)\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Classify an agent exit code into an `Outcome`, with shutdown signal support.\n///\n/// Delegates to `Outcome::classify()`. Provided here for ergonomic imports.\npub fn classify(exit_code: i32, was_interrupted: bool) -> Outcome {\n    Outcome::classify(exit_code, was_interrupted)\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// OutcomeHandler\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Routes agent outcomes to their explicit handlers.\npub struct OutcomeHandler {\n    config: Config,\n    telemetry: Telemetry,\n}\n\nimpl OutcomeHandler {\n    pub fn new(config: Config, telemetry: Telemetry) -> Self {\n        OutcomeHandler { config, telemetry }\n    }\n\n    /// Run a bead store operation with a 30s timeout.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `Ok(Some(T))` on success, `Ok(None)` on timeout, and `Err(E)` on\n    /// other errors. Callers should treat timeout and error as non-fatal — log\n    /// and continue rather than blocking the worker in HANDLING state.\n    async fn timeout_op<T, F, Fut>(\n        &self,\n        op: F,\n        operation_name: &str,\n    ) -> Result<Option<T>, anyhow::Error>\n    where\n        F: FnOnce() -> Fut,\n        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = Result<T, anyhow::Error>>,\n    {\n        match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), op()).await {\n            Ok(Ok(result)) => Ok(Some(result)),\n            Ok(Err(e)) => Err(e),\n            Err(_) => {\n                tracing::error!(\n                    operation = operation_name,\n                    \"bead store operation timed out after 30s\"\n                );\n                Ok(None)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Release a bead with sync conflict recovery and graceful failure handling.\n    ///\n    /// Flow:\n    /// 1. Run `br sync --flush-only` to push local writes to JSONL first.\n    /// 2. Attempt `br update` to release the bead (set status=open, assignee=\"\").\n    /// 3. If SYNC_CONFLICT recovery fails, emit `BeadReleaseFailed` and return Ok.\n    ///\n    /// This method never returns an error — release failures are non-fatal.\n    /// The worker can continue processing other beads even if release fails.\n    async fn release_bead(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore, bead: &Bead) -> Result<Vec<EventKind>> {\n        let mut events = Vec::new();\n\n        // Step 1: Flush local writes to JSONL before attempting release.\n        // Emit heartbeat before br call to track where hang occurs.\n        let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted {\n            bead_id: Some(bead.id.clone()),\n            state: \"HANDLING_FLUSH\".to_string(),\n        });\n\n        match self.timeout_op(|| store.flush(), \"flush\").await {\n            Ok(Some(())) => {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"flushed local changes to JSONL before release\"\n                );\n                // Emit heartbeat after successful flush.\n                let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted {\n                    bead_id: Some(bead.id.clone()),\n                    state: \"HANDLING_FLUSH_DONE\".to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n            Ok(None) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"flush timed out before release, attempting release anyway\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    outcome: \"release\".to_string(),\n                    operation: \"flush\".to_string(),\n                    error: \"timeout after 30s\".to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"flush failed before release, attempting release anyway\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    outcome: \"release\".to_string(),\n                    operation: \"flush\".to_string(),\n                    error: e.to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Step 2: Attempt release with timeout protection.\n        // Emit heartbeat before br release call to track where hang occurs.\n        let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted {\n            bead_id: Some(bead.id.clone()),\n            state: \"HANDLING_RELEASE\".to_string(),\n        });\n\n        let release_result =\n            tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), store.release(&bead.id)).await;\n\n        match release_result {\n            Ok(Ok(())) => {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"bead released successfully\"\n                );\n                // Emit heartbeat after successful release.\n                let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::HeartbeatEmitted {\n                    bead_id: Some(bead.id.clone()),\n                    state: \"HANDLING_RELEASE_DONE\".to_string(),\n                });\n                events.push(EventKind::BeadReleased {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    reason: \"release_success\".to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                // Release failed (br error, not timeout).\n                // Check if this is a SyncRecoveryError (sync recovery was attempted but failed).\n                if e.downcast_ref::<SyncRecoveryError>().is_some() {\n                    tracing::error!(\n                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"br release failed after SYNC_CONFLICT recovery — \\\n                         worker will continue (bead may remain assigned)\"\n                    );\n                } else {\n                    tracing::error!(\n                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"br release failed — worker will continue (bead may remain assigned)\"\n                    );\n                }\n                events.push(EventKind::BeadReleaseFailed {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    reason: e.to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                // Timeout after 30s.\n                tracing::error!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"br release timed out after 30s — worker will continue (bead may remain assigned)\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::BeadReleaseFailed {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    reason: \"timeout after 30s\".to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(events)\n    }\n\n    /// Handle a process output for the given bead.\n    ///\n    /// Uses `classify()` to determine the outcome, then dispatches to the\n    /// per-outcome handler. Every `Outcome` variant has an explicit arm —\n    /// no wildcards. Returns a `HandlerResult` describing what happened.\n    #[tracing::instrument(\n        name = \"bead.outcome\",\n        skip(self, store, bead, output),\n        fields(\n            needle.bead.id = %bead.id,\n            needle.outcome = tracing::field::Empty,\n            needle.outcome.action = tracing::field::Empty,\n        )\n    )]\n    pub async fn handle(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        output: &AgentOutcome,\n        was_interrupted: bool,\n    ) -> Result<HandlerResult> {\n        let outcome = classify(output.exit_code, was_interrupted);\n\n        // Set outcome as span attribute\n        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.outcome\", outcome.as_str());\n\n        tracing::info!(\n            bead_id = %bead.id,\n            exit_code = output.exit_code,\n            outcome = %outcome,\n            \"handling agent outcome\"\n        );\n\n        // Use emit_try_lock() to avoid blocking if telemetry writer is stuck.\n        // This prevents worker hang in HANDLING state when telemetry is wedged.\n        let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::OutcomeClassified {\n            bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n            outcome: outcome.as_str().to_string(),\n            exit_code: output.exit_code,\n        });\n\n        let (bead_action, telemetry_events) = match outcome.clone() {\n            Outcome::Success => self.handle_success(store, bead).await?,\n            Outcome::Failure => self.handle_failure(store, bead).await?,\n            Outcome::Timeout => self.handle_timeout(store, bead).await?,\n            Outcome::AgentNotFound => self.handle_agent_not_found(store, bead).await?,\n            Outcome::Interrupted => self.handle_interrupted(store, bead).await?,\n            Outcome::Crash(code) => self.handle_crash(store, bead, code).await?,\n        };\n\n        // Emit sub-handler events (e.g. BeadCompleted, BeadOrphaned) to the\n        // telemetry sink so they appear in the JSONL log.\n        // Use emit_try_lock() to avoid blocking if telemetry writer is stuck.\n        for event in &telemetry_events {\n            let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(event.clone());\n        }\n\n        // Use emit_try_lock() to avoid blocking if telemetry writer is stuck.\n        let _ = self.telemetry.emit_try_lock(EventKind::OutcomeHandled {\n            bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n            outcome: outcome.as_str().to_string(),\n            action: bead_action.to_string(),\n        });\n\n        // Set action as span attribute\n        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.outcome.action\", bead_action.to_string());\n\n        // Set span status: Ok for success, Error otherwise\n        if !matches!(outcome, Outcome::Success) {\n            tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_code\", 2u64);\n            tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_description\", outcome.as_str());\n        }\n\n        Ok(HandlerResult {\n            outcome,\n            bead_action,\n            telemetry_events,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Handle a process output with cancellation support.\n    ///\n    /// Checks the cancellation flag before starting the handler and returns\n    /// early if the handler has been cancelled (e.g., due to a timeout in\n    /// the worker). This prevents the handler from making further br calls\n    /// after a timeout has occurred.\n    pub async fn handle_with_cancellation(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        output: &AgentOutcome,\n        was_interrupted: bool,\n        cancelled: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n    ) -> Result<HandlerResult> {\n        // Check if we've been cancelled before starting.\n        if cancelled.load(Ordering::Acquire) {\n            tracing::warn!(\n                bead_id = %bead.id,\n                \"outcome handler cancelled before starting, returning early\"\n            );\n            // Return a default HandlerResult to allow the worker to recover.\n            return Ok(HandlerResult {\n                outcome: classify(output.exit_code, was_interrupted),\n                bead_action: BeadAction::None,\n                telemetry_events: vec![],\n            });\n        }\n\n        // Wrap the handler in a timeout to prevent indefinite hangs.\n        // This is a safety net in case the internal br call timeouts don't work.\n        let bead_id = bead.id.clone();\n        let telemetry = self.telemetry.clone();\n\n        match tokio::time::timeout(\n            std::time::Duration::from_secs(50),\n            self.handle(store, bead, output, was_interrupted),\n        )\n        .await\n        {\n            Ok(Ok(result)) => Ok(result),\n            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                // Handler returned an error.\n                tracing::error!(\n                    bead_id = %bead_id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"outcome handler returned error\"\n                );\n                Err(e)\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                // Timeout after 50 seconds.\n                tracing::error!(\n                    bead_id = %bead_id,\n                    \"outcome handler timed out after 50s, returning early to allow worker recovery\"\n                );\n                // Emit a timeout event for observability.\n                let _ = telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                    bead_id: bead_id.clone(),\n                    outcome: classify(output.exit_code, was_interrupted)\n                        .as_str()\n                        .to_string(),\n                    operation: \"handle\".to_string(),\n                    error: \"timeout after 50s\".to_string(),\n                });\n                // Return a default HandlerResult to allow the worker to recover.\n                // The worker's 60-second timeout wrapper will handle best-effort release.\n                Ok(HandlerResult {\n                    outcome: classify(output.exit_code, was_interrupted),\n                    bead_action: BeadAction::Released,\n                    telemetry_events: vec![EventKind::BeadReleased {\n                        bead_id,\n                        reason: \"handler_timeout\".to_string(),\n                    }],\n                })\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Success: run validation gates (if configured), then verify bead closure.\n    ///\n    /// Flow:\n    /// 1. If validation gates are configured (new or legacy format), run them.\n    /// 2. If any gate fails: reopen the bead (if agent closed it) and release it.\n    /// 3. If all gates pass (or none configured): check if agent closed the bead.\n    ///    - Closed → emit BeadCompleted.\n    ///    - Still open → emit BeadOrphaned warning.\n    ///\n    /// NEEDLE does NOT auto-close — the agent owns closure via `br close`.\n    async fn handle_success(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n        tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent completed successfully\");\n\n        // Try pluggable gates first, fall back to legacy verification commands.\n        let gate = if !self.config.gates.is_empty() {\n            // New pluggable gate system.\n            let gate_configs: Vec<(String, GateConfig)> = self\n                .config\n                .gates\n                .iter()\n                .enumerate()\n                .map(|(i, config)| (format!(\"gate_{}\", i), config.clone()))\n                .collect();\n            ValidationGate::new(gate_configs, bead.workspace.clone())\n        } else if !self.config.verification.is_empty() {\n            // Legacy verification command format.\n            ValidationGate::from_commands(self.config.verification.clone(), bead.workspace.clone())\n        } else {\n            None\n        };\n\n        if let Some(gate) = gate {\n            let report = gate.run(bead).await?;\n\n            if !report.all_passed {\n                return self.handle_gate_failure(store, bead, &report).await;\n            }\n\n            // All gates passed — emit telemetry.\n            let gates_run = report.results.len() as u32;\n            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::VerificationPassed {\n                bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                gates_run,\n            })?;\n            tracing::info!(\n                bead_id = %bead.id,\n                gates_run,\n                \"all validation gates passed\"\n            );\n        }\n\n        // Reset failure count on successful gate completion.\n        // This ensures the bead starts fresh on the next cycle if re-claimed.\n        let _ = self.reset_failure_count(store, bead).await;\n\n        // Normal success flow: check if agent closed the bead.\n        let mut events = Vec::new();\n\n        // Use timeout for show() to prevent indefinite hang in HANDLING state.\n        match self.timeout_op(|| store.show(&bead.id), \"show\").await {\n            Ok(Some(current)) if current.status.is_done() => {\n                tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"bead confirmed closed by agent\");\n                events.push(EventKind::BeadCompleted {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    duration_ms: 0,\n                });\n                // Increment success_count for any skills that matched this bead.\n                if !bead.workspace.as_os_str().is_empty() {\n                    if let Ok(lib) = crate::skill::SkillLibrary::load(&bead.workspace) {\n                        if let Err(e) = lib.increment_success_for_bead(&bead.labels, &bead.title) {\n                            tracing::warn!(\n                                bead_id = %bead.id,\n                                error = %e,\n                                \"failed to increment skill success counts\"\n                            );\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(Some(current)) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    status = %current.status,\n                    \"agent exited successfully but bead is still open (orphaned)\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::BeadOrphaned {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                });\n            }\n            Ok(None) => {\n                // Timeout - emit telemetry and continue.\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"show() timed out, could not verify bead closure status\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    outcome: \"success\".to_string(),\n                    operation: \"show\".to_string(),\n                    error: \"timeout after 30s\".to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"could not verify bead closure status\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    outcome: \"success\".to_string(),\n                    operation: \"show\".to_string(),\n                    error: e.to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Flush SQLite state to JSONL so the closed/orphaned bead is captured in\n        // the git-committed backup. Non-fatal: a flush failure is logged but does\n        // not block the worker from picking up the next bead.\n        match self.timeout_op(|| store.flush(), \"flush\").await {\n            Ok(Some(())) => {\n                tracing::debug!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"flushed bead state to JSONL after success\");\n            }\n            Ok(None) => {\n                tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"flush timed out after success — JSONL may lag SQLite\");\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, error = %e, \"flush failed after success — JSONL may lag SQLite\");\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok((BeadAction::None, events))\n    }\n\n    /// Handle gate failure: reopen the bead if it was closed, then release it.\n    async fn handle_gate_failure(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        report: &crate::validation::GateReport,\n    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n        // Find the first failing gate for telemetry.\n        let (failed_gate, reason) = report\n            .results\n            .iter()\n            .find(|(_, r)| !r.passed())\n            .map(|(name, r)| (name.clone(), r.failure_reason().unwrap_or(\"\").to_string()))\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| (\"unknown\".to_string(), \"unknown error\".to_string()));\n\n        tracing::warn!(\n            bead_id = %bead.id,\n            gate = %failed_gate,\n            reason = %reason,\n            \"validation gate failed — releasing bead\"\n        );\n\n        // Emit verification failure telemetry.\n        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::VerificationFailed {\n            bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n            command: failed_gate.clone(),\n            exit_code: None,\n            output: reason,\n        })?;\n\n        let mut events = Vec::new();\n\n        // If the agent already closed the bead, reopen it before releasing.\n        // Use timeout to prevent indefinite hang in HANDLING state.\n        match self.timeout_op(|| store.show(&bead.id), \"show\").await {\n            Ok(Some(current)) if current.status.is_done() => {\n                tracing::info!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"reopening bead closed by agent (verification failed)\"\n                );\n                match self.timeout_op(|| store.reopen(&bead.id), \"reopen\").await {\n                    Ok(_) => {}\n                    Err(e) => {\n                        tracing::warn!(\n                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n                            error = %e,\n                            \"failed to reopen bead — attempting release anyway\"\n                        );\n                        events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                            bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                            outcome: \"gate_failure\".to_string(),\n                            operation: \"reopen\".to_string(),\n                            error: e.to_string(),\n                        });\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(None) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"show() timed out during gate failure handling, skipping reopen\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    outcome: \"gate_failure\".to_string(),\n                    operation: \"show\".to_string(),\n                    error: \"timeout after 30s\".to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"show() failed during gate failure handling\"\n                );\n            }\n            _ => {}\n        }\n\n        // Release the bead back to open with flush-before-release and sync recovery.\n        let mut release_events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n        events.append(&mut release_events);\n\n        // If release succeeded, increment failure count.\n        let release_succeeded = events\n            .iter()\n            .any(|e| matches!(e, EventKind::BeadReleased { .. }));\n        if release_succeeded {\n            if let Err(e) = self.increment_failure_count(store, bead).await {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to increment failure count after gate failure\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Add a label indicating verification failure.\n        if let Err(e) = tokio::time::timeout(\n            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n            store.add_label(&bead.id, \"verification-failed\"),\n        )\n        .await\n        {\n            tracing::warn!(\n                bead_id = %bead.id,\n                error = %e,\n                \"failed to add verification-failed label\"\n            );\n        }\n\n        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n    }\n\n    /// Failure: release bead and increment failure count.\n    ///\n    /// Mitosis evaluation (for multi-task splitting) is handled externally by\n    /// the worker after outcome handling — see `MitosisEvaluator`.\n    ///\n    /// If br calls timeout or fail, logs the error and continues — does not\n    /// block the worker in HANDLING state indefinitely.\n    async fn handle_failure(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n        tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent failure — releasing bead\");\n\n        let mut events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n\n        // If release succeeded, increment failure count.\n        // Check if the last event is BeadReleased (success) or BeadReleaseFailed (failure).\n        let release_succeeded = events\n            .iter()\n            .any(|e| matches!(e, EventKind::BeadReleased { .. }));\n        if release_succeeded {\n            if let Err(e) = self.increment_failure_count(store, bead).await {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to increment failure count after release\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Ensure we emit a reason event for telemetry.\n        if !events.iter().any(|e| {\n            matches!(\n                e,\n                EventKind::BeadReleased { .. } | EventKind::BeadReleaseFailed { .. }\n            )\n        }) {\n            events.push(EventKind::BeadReleased {\n                bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                reason: \"failure\".to_string(),\n            });\n        }\n\n        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n    }\n\n    /// Timeout: release bead and add `deferred` label.\n    ///\n    /// If br calls timeout or fail, logs the error and continues — does not\n    /// block the worker in HANDLING state indefinitely.\n    async fn handle_timeout(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n        tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent timed out — releasing bead as deferred\");\n\n        let mut events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n\n        // If release succeeded, increment failure count and add deferred label.\n        let release_succeeded = events\n            .iter()\n            .any(|e| matches!(e, EventKind::BeadReleased { .. }));\n        if release_succeeded {\n            // Increment failure count for auto-split tracking.\n            if let Err(e) = self.increment_failure_count(store, bead).await {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to increment failure count after timeout\"\n                );\n            }\n\n            if let Err(e) = tokio::time::timeout(\n                std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n                store.add_label(&bead.id, \"deferred\"),\n            )\n            .await\n            {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"add_label deferred timed out or failed after timeout release\"\n                );\n                events.push(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    outcome: \"timeout\".to_string(),\n                    operation: \"add_label\".to_string(),\n                    error: e.to_string(),\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok((BeadAction::Deferred, events))\n    }\n\n    /// Crash: release bead and create alert bead with diagnostic info.\n    async fn handle_crash(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        signal_code: i32,\n    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n        tracing::error!(\n            bead_id = %bead.id,\n            signal_code,\n            agent = %self.config.agent.default,\n            \"agent crashed — releasing bead and creating alert\"\n        );\n\n        let events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n\n        // Create alert bead with diagnostic info (best-effort).\n        let signal_num = if signal_code > 128 {\n            signal_code - 128\n        } else {\n            signal_code\n        };\n        let timestamp = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();\n        let alert_title = format!(\"ALERT: Agent crash on bead {}\", bead.id);\n        let alert_body = format!(\n            \"## Agent Crash Report\\n\\\n             \\n\\\n             - **Bead ID**: {}\\n\\\n             - **Agent**: {}\\n\\\n             - **Exit code**: {} (signal {})\\n\\\n             - **Workspace**: {}\\n\\\n             - **Timestamp**: {}\\n\\\n             \\n\\\n             The agent process was killed. This bead has been released for retry.\",\n            bead.id,\n            self.config.agent.default,\n            signal_code,\n            signal_num,\n            bead.workspace.display(),\n            timestamp,\n        );\n\n        // Hook 4: propagate stitch labels from the crashed bead to the alert.\n        let signal_label = format!(\"signal-{}\", signal_num);\n        let mut alert_labels: Vec<String> = vec![\"alert\".into(), \"crash\".into(), signal_label];\n        alert_labels.extend(crate::types::extract_stitch_labels(&bead.labels));\n        let alert_label_refs: Vec<&str> = alert_labels.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();\n        match tokio::time::timeout(\n            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n            store.create_bead(&alert_title, &alert_body, &alert_label_refs),\n        )\n        .await\n        {\n            Ok(Ok(alert_id)) => {\n                tracing::info!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    %alert_id,\n                    \"crash alert bead created\"\n                );\n            }\n            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to create crash alert bead\"\n                );\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"create_bead timed out after 30s during crash handling\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok((BeadAction::Alerted, events))\n    }\n\n    /// AgentNotFound: release bead, emit error. No retry — this is a config issue.\n    async fn handle_agent_not_found(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n        tracing::error!(\n            bead_id = %bead.id,\n            agent = %self.config.agent.default,\n            \"agent binary not found — releasing bead (config issue, no retry)\"\n        );\n\n        let events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n    }\n\n    /// Interrupted: release bead for graceful shutdown.\n    async fn handle_interrupted(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n    ) -> Result<(BeadAction, Vec<EventKind>)> {\n        tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent interrupted — releasing bead for clean shutdown\");\n\n        let events = self.release_bead(store, bead).await?;\n        Ok((BeadAction::Released, events))\n    }\n\n    /// Increment the failure count label on a bead.\n    ///\n    /// Labels follow the pattern `failure-count:N`. If `failure-count:2` exists,\n    /// the old label is removed and `failure-count:3` is added.\n    ///\n    /// All `br` calls are wrapped in timeouts to prevent indefinite hang in\n    /// HANDLING state. Failures are non-fatal — we log and continue.\n    async fn increment_failure_count(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore, bead: &Bead) -> Result<()> {\n        // Read labels with timeout.\n        let labels =\n            match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), store.labels(&bead.id))\n                .await\n            {\n                Ok(Ok(l)) => l,\n                Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"could not read labels to increment failure count\"\n                    );\n                    return Ok(());\n                }\n                Err(_) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n                        \"labels() timed out after 30s, skipping failure count increment\"\n                    );\n                    return Ok(());\n                }\n            };\n\n        let current_count = labels\n            .iter()\n            .filter_map(|l| l.strip_prefix(\"failure-count:\"))\n            .filter_map(|n| n.parse::<u32>().ok())\n            .max()\n            .unwrap_or(0);\n\n        let new_count = current_count + 1;\n        let new_label = format!(\"failure-count:{}\", new_count);\n\n        // Remove old failure-count labels before adding the new one.\n        // Each remove_label call is wrapped in a timeout.\n        for label in &labels {\n            if label.starts_with(\"failure-count:\") {\n                match tokio::time::timeout(\n                    std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n                    store.remove_label(&bead.id, label),\n                )\n                .await\n                {\n                    Ok(Ok(())) => {}\n                    Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                        tracing::warn!(\n                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n                            label,\n                            error = %e,\n                            \"failed to remove old failure-count label\"\n                        );\n                    }\n                    Err(_) => {\n                        tracing::warn!(\n                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n                            label,\n                            \"remove_label timed out after 30s\"\n                        );\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Add the new label with timeout.\n        match tokio::time::timeout(\n            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n            store.add_label(&bead.id, &new_label),\n        )\n        .await\n        {\n            Ok(Ok(())) => {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    count = new_count,\n                    \"failure count incremented\"\n                );\n            }\n            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to add failure-count label\"\n                );\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"add_label timed out after 30s\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Reset the failure count label on a bead by removing all `failure-count:N` labels.\n    ///\n    /// Called on success to clear the failure counter so the bead starts fresh\n    /// on the next cycle.\n    ///\n    /// All `br` calls are wrapped in timeouts to prevent indefinite hang in\n    /// HANDLING state. Failures are non-fatal — we log and continue.\n    async fn reset_failure_count(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore, bead: &Bead) -> Result<()> {\n        // Read labels with timeout.\n        let labels =\n            match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), store.labels(&bead.id))\n                .await\n            {\n                Ok(Ok(l)) => l,\n                Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"could not read labels to reset failure count\"\n                    );\n                    return Ok(());\n                }\n                Err(_) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        bead_id = %bead.id,\n                        \"labels() timed out after 30s, skipping failure count reset\"\n                    );\n                    return Ok(());\n                }\n            };\n\n        // Remove all failure-count labels.\n        let mut removed_count = 0;\n        for label in &labels {\n            if label.starts_with(\"failure-count:\") {\n                match tokio::time::timeout(\n                    std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n                    store.remove_label(&bead.id, label),\n                )\n                .await\n                {\n                    Ok(Ok(())) => {\n                        removed_count += 1;\n                    }\n                    Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                        tracing::warn!(\n                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n                            label,\n                            error = %e,\n                            \"failed to remove failure-count label\"\n                        );\n                    }\n                    Err(_) => {\n                        tracing::warn!(\n                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n                            label,\n                            \"remove_label timed out after 30s\"\n                        );\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if removed_count > 0 {\n            tracing::debug!(\n                bead_id = %bead.id,\n                removed_count,\n                \"failure count reset\"\n            );\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Outcome Display\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nimpl fmt::Display for Outcome {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            Outcome::Success => write!(f, \"Success\"),\n            Outcome::Failure => write!(f, \"Failure\"),\n            Outcome::Timeout => write!(f, \"Timeout\"),\n            Outcome::AgentNotFound => write!(f, \"AgentNotFound\"),\n            Outcome::Interrupted => write!(f, \"Interrupted\"),\n            Outcome::Crash(code) => write!(f, \"Crash({})\", code),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//
